# 逐文件修改记录

本记录只覆盖当前主控和微动板；通用Bootloader已迁入独立仓库，不再从本目录生成其修改记录。

已按功能补充中文注释及主控F0串口升级入口；本次删除主控旧RGB专用模块，将原扭蛋通道改为第二路出珠且暂不调用。

修改基线为本目录Git初始提交8ecfd8c。功能和安卓协议依据只读参考工程 Wusong_New_1.7_fixed；原项目及General_KT均未写入。

以下每项给出绝对路径、模块/函数、原因及影响范围；diff中的减号行是原代码，加号行是修改后代码，空格行是上下文。生产源码仅修改已有文件，新增文件限于说明、输出固件和验证材料。

## 1. Wusong/Core/Inc/main.h

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Core\Inc\main.h`

模块/函数：主控引脚宏 / PlayButton5/6、Serial2。

修改原因：补齐PD12/PD13两路按键，明确PD5/PD6用途。

影响范围：六路直连按键和球盘串口。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Core/Inc/main.h b/Wusong/Core/Inc/main.h
index 2b63bdd..2ee4fce 100644
--- a/Wusong/Core/Inc/main.h
+++ b/Wusong/Core/Inc/main.h
@@ -135,7 +135,7 @@ void Error_Handler(void);
 #define Tube_SER_Pin GPIO_PIN_15
 #define Tube_SER_GPIO_Port GPIOB

-/* 中文注释：四路外接游玩/拍拍按键，协议编号依次为1、2、3、4；按键4正式使用PD11。 */
+/* 六路直连游玩/拍拍按键使用PD8~PD13，对外编号依次为1~6。 */
 #define PlayButton1_Pin GPIO_PIN_8
 #define PlayButton1_GPIO_Port GPIOD
 #define PlayButton2_Pin GPIO_PIN_9
@@ -145,7 +145,12 @@ void Error_Handler(void);
 #define PlayButton4_Pin GPIO_PIN_11
 #define PlayButton4_GPIO_Port GPIOD

-/* 中文注释：PD5/PD6按新原理图保留给串口2，通信参数后续确定后再初始化USART2。 */
+#define PlayButton5_Pin GPIO_PIN_12
+#define PlayButton5_GPIO_Port GPIOD
+#define PlayButton6_Pin GPIO_PIN_13
+#define PlayButton6_GPIO_Port GPIOD
+
+/* PD5/PD6使用USART2与球盘微动板通信，115200 8N1。 */
 #define Serial2_TX_Pin GPIO_PIN_5
 #define Serial2_TX_GPIO_Port GPIOD
 #define Serial2_RX_Pin GPIO_PIN_6
```

## 4. Wusong/Core/Src/gpio.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Core\Src\gpio.c`

模块/函数：GPIO / MX_GPIO_Init。

修改原因：PD12/PD13并入游玩按键输入初始化；电子锁注释去除错误机型名称，PA0逻辑不变。

影响范围：六路直连按键及电子锁注释。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Core/Src/gpio.c b/Wusong/Core/Src/gpio.c
index d2cd149..dc5d73c 100644
--- a/Wusong/Core/Src/gpio.c
+++ b/Wusong/Core/Src/gpio.c
@@ -67,7 +67,7 @@ void MX_GPIO_Init(void)
   HAL_GPIO_WritePin(CardOutput_GPIO_Port, CardOutput_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
   HAL_GPIO_WritePin(Card2Output_GPIO_Port, Card2Output_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);

-  /* 中文注释：新扭蛋机原理图中电子锁控制为 PA0，默认输出低电平保持关闭。 */
+  /* 中文注释：电子锁控制为PA0，默认输出低电平保持关闭。 */
   HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOA, GPIO_PIN_0, GPIO_PIN_RESET);

   /* 中文注释：RCLK1经过74HC14反相，MCU侧默认拉高可使SM16306的LE保持低电平锁存；SEG_OE先拉高关闭输出。 */
@@ -136,8 +136,8 @@ void MX_GPIO_Init(void)
   GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
   HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOB, &GPIO_InitStruct);

-  /* 中文注释：后台设置按键K3使用PD0；游玩按键1~4使用PD8/PD9/PD10/PD11，均由板上电阻上拉。 */
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = KeyBoard1_Pin | PlayButton1_Pin | PlayButton2_Pin | PlayButton3_Pin | PlayButton4_Pin;
+  /* 中文注释：后台设置按键K3使用PD0；游玩按键1~6使用PD8~PD13，均由板上电阻上拉。 */
+  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = KeyBoard1_Pin | PlayButton1_Pin | PlayButton2_Pin | PlayButton3_Pin | PlayButton4_Pin | PlayButton5_Pin | PlayButton6_Pin;
   GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
   GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
   HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOD, &GPIO_InitStruct);
```

## 5. Wusong/Core/Src/main.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Core\Src\main.c`

模块/函数：启动 / main。

修改原因：初始化USART2；HAL_Init前调用SystemCoreClockUpdate，按Boot继承的实际RCC配置建立毫秒时基。

影响范围：主控球盘链路及上电/Boot跳转时钟交接；SWD冷启动同样适用。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Core/Src/main.c b/Wusong/Core/Src/main.c
index a958926..0b79529 100644
--- a/Wusong/Core/Src/main.c
+++ b/Wusong/Core/Src/main.c
@@ -69,6 +69,8 @@ int main(void)
 {

   /* USER CODE BEGIN 1 */
+  /* Boot可能保留168MHz；先读取实际时钟，避免HAL_Init按默认16MHz配置SysTick。 */
+  SystemCoreClockUpdate();
   __enable_irq();
   /* USER CODE END 1 */

@@ -97,8 +99,9 @@ int main(void)
   MX_TIM7_Init();
   MX_IWDG_Init();
   MX_USART3_UART_Init();
+  MX_USART2_UART_Init();
   /* USER CODE BEGIN 2 */
-  /* 中文注释：USART1用于安卓通信，USART3使用PB10/PB11连接独立控台，均为115200 8N1。 */
+  /* 中文注释：USART1连接安卓，USART3使用PB10/PB11连接控台，USART2使用PD5/PD6连接微动板，均为115200 8N1。 */
   Main_Init();
   /* USER CODE END 2 */
```

## 7. Wusong/Core/Src/system_stm32f4xx.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Core\Src\system_stm32f4xx.c`

模块/函数：向量表 / SystemInit、USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS、VECT_TAB_OFFSET。

修改原因：显式设置Flash向量偏移0xC000。

影响范围：与当前Bootloader及已有链接地址一致，支持调试器和Bootloader两种启动。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Core/Src/system_stm32f4xx.c b/Wusong/Core/Src/system_stm32f4xx.c
index 7a61e9c..8f05901 100644
--- a/Wusong/Core/Src/system_stm32f4xx.c
+++ b/Wusong/Core/Src/system_stm32f4xx.c
@@ -91,7 +91,8 @@
 /*!< Uncomment the following line if you need to relocate the vector table
      anywhere in Flash or Sram, else the vector table is kept at the automatic
      remap of boot address selected */
-/* #define USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS */
+/* 应用与Bootloader共存，向量表必须与Keil应用起始地址0x0800C000一致。 */
+#define USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS

 #if defined(USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS)
 /*!< Uncomment the following line if you need to relocate your vector Table
@@ -105,7 +106,7 @@
                                                      This value must be a multiple of 0x200. */
 #endif /* VECT_TAB_SRAM */
 #if !defined(VECT_TAB_OFFSET)
-#define VECT_TAB_OFFSET         0x00000000U     /*!< Vector Table offset field.
+#define VECT_TAB_OFFSET         0x0000C000U     /*!< Vector Table offset field.
                                                      This value must be a multiple of 0x200. */
 #endif /* VECT_TAB_OFFSET */
 #endif /* USER_VECT_TAB_ADDRESS */
```

## 8. Wusong/Core/Src/usart.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Core\Src\usart.c`

模块/函数：串口驱动 / MX_USART2_UART_Init、HAL_UART_MspInit/DeInit。

修改原因：新增115200 8N1的USART2、PD5/PD6 AF7及NVIC配置。

影响范围：仅增加球盘链路；USART1/3引脚不变。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Core/Src/usart.c b/Wusong/Core/Src/usart.c
index b9f1e8c..f124738 100644
--- a/Wusong/Core/Src/usart.c
+++ b/Wusong/Core/Src/usart.c
@@ -26,6 +26,7 @@

 UART_HandleTypeDef huart5;
 UART_HandleTypeDef huart1;
+UART_HandleTypeDef huart2;
 UART_HandleTypeDef huart3;

 /* UART5 init function */
@@ -115,6 +116,21 @@ void MX_USART3_UART_Init(void)

 }

+/* 主控USART2连接微动板，保持与对端USART1一致的115200、8位、无校验、1停止位。 */
+void MX_USART2_UART_Init(void)
+{
+  huart2.Instance = USART2;
+  huart2.Init.BaudRate = 115200;
+  huart2.Init.WordLength = UART_WORDLENGTH_8B;
+  huart2.Init.StopBits = UART_STOPBITS_1;
+  huart2.Init.Parity = UART_PARITY_NONE;
+  huart2.Init.Mode = UART_MODE_TX_RX;
+  huart2.Init.HwFlowCtl = UART_HWCONTROL_NONE;
+  huart2.Init.OverSampling = UART_OVERSAMPLING_16;
+  if (HAL_UART_Init(&huart2) != HAL_OK)
+    Error_Handler();
+}
+
 void HAL_UART_MspInit(UART_HandleTypeDef* uartHandle)
 {

@@ -185,6 +201,20 @@ void HAL_UART_MspInit(UART_HandleTypeDef* uartHandle)

   /* USER CODE END USART1_MspInit 1 */
   }
+  else if(uartHandle->Instance==USART2)
+  {
+    __HAL_RCC_USART2_CLK_ENABLE();
+    __HAL_RCC_GPIOD_CLK_ENABLE();
+    /* PD5=TX、PD6=RX，使用AF7；接收由USART2中断逐字节送入环形缓冲。 */
+    GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = Serial2_TX_Pin | Serial2_RX_Pin;
+    GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_AF_PP;
+    GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_PULLUP;
+    GPIO_InitStruct.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_VERY_HIGH;
+    GPIO_InitStruct.Alternate = GPIO_AF7_USART2;
+    HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOD, &GPIO_InitStruct);
+    HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(USART2_IRQn, 0, 0);
+    HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(USART2_IRQn);
+  }
   else if(uartHandle->Instance==USART3)
   {
   /* USER CODE BEGIN USART3_MspInit 0 */
@@ -257,6 +287,12 @@ void HAL_UART_MspDeInit(UART_HandleTypeDef* uartHandle)

   /* USER CODE END USART1_MspDeInit 1 */
   }
+  else if(uartHandle->Instance==USART2)
+  {
+    __HAL_RCC_USART2_CLK_DISABLE();
+    HAL_GPIO_DeInit(GPIOD, Serial2_TX_Pin | Serial2_RX_Pin);
+    HAL_NVIC_DisableIRQ(USART2_IRQn);
+  }
   else if(uartHandle->Instance==USART3)
   {
   /* USER CODE BEGIN USART3_MspDeInit 0 */
```

```diff
-extern RGB_t Color_table[];

-void RGB_Init(Light_t *light, TIM_HandleTypeDef *htim, uint32_t Channel, uint16_t LED_NUM, RGB_t *RGB_Buffer, uint16_t *CRR_Buffer, Semaphore_t *Semaphore, RGB_Order Order);
```

影响范围：仅主控旧RGB专用模块；实际12路通道灯位于微动板，不受影响。

```diff
-    {
-        {255, 0, 0},        // 红色
-        {0, 255, 0},        // 绿色
-        {0, 0, 255},        // 蓝色
-        {0, 255, 255},      // 天蓝色

-/*

-/*
- * @brief  将RGB颜色转换为RGB结构体
- * @param  R 红色通道数值0-255
- * @param  G 绿色通道数值0-255
- * @param  B 蓝色通道数值0-255
- * @retval RGB颜色结构体
- */
-RGB_t RGB_Color(uint8_t R, uint8_t G, uint8_t B)
-{
-    RGB_t color;
-    color.R = R;
-    color.G = G;
-    color.B = B;
-    return color;
-}
-
-/*
- * @brief  设置单个灯珠的RGB颜色
- * @param  LED_ID:灯珠序号id
- * @param  color:RGB颜色结构体
- * @param  lightness:亮度0~255
- * @retval none
- */
-void RGB_SetOneColor(Light_t *light, uint16_t LED_ID, RGB_t color, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness)
-{
-
-    if (LED_ID > light->LED_NUM)
-        return;
-    light->RGB_buffer[LED_ID].R = color.R * RelativeLightness * AbsoluteLightness / 2550;
-    light->RGB_buffer[LED_ID].G = color.G * RelativeLightness * AbsoluteLightness / 2550;
-    light->RGB_buffer[LED_ID].B = color.B * RelativeLightness * AbsoluteLightness / 2550;
-}
-
-/*
- * @brief  设置指定范围的灯珠颜色
- * @param  start:起始位置
- * @param  end:结束位置
- * @param  color:RGB颜色结构体
- * @param  lightness:亮度0~255
- * @retval none
- */
-void RGB_SetMoreColor(Light_t *lihgt, uint16_t start, uint16_t end, RGB_t color, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness)
-{
-    for (uint16_t i = start; i <= end; i++)
-    {
-        RGB_SetOneColor(lihgt, i, color, AbsoluteLightness, RelativeLightness);
-    }
-}
-
-/*
- * @brief  设置指定灯光区域内的所有灯珠颜色
- * @param  color:颜色结构体
- * @param  lightness:亮度0~255
- * @retval none
- */
-void RGB_SetAllColor(Light_t *light, RGB_t color, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness)
-{
-    RGB_SetMoreColor(light, 0, light->LED_NUM, color, AbsoluteLightness, RelativeLightness);
-}
-void RGB_CleanAll(Light_t *light)
-{
-    RGB_SetAllColor(light, NONE, 0, 0);
-    memset(light->RGB_buffer, 0, light->LED_NUM * sizeof(RGB_t));
-    memset(light->CRR_buffer, code0, (light->LED_NUM + 7) * 24);
-}
-
-bool SemaphoreTake(Semaphore_t *Semaphore)
-{
-    if (Semaphore->num == 0)
-        return false;
-    else
-        Semaphore->num = 0;
-    return true;
-}
-
-bool SemaphoreGive(Semaphore_t *Semaphore)
-{
-    if (Semaphore->num == 1)
-        return false;
-    else
-        Semaphore->num = 1;
-    return true;
-}
-
-void RGB_FinishCallback(Light_t *light, DMA_HandleTypeDef *hdma)
-{
-    if (__HAL_DMA_GET_IT_SOURCE(hdma, DMA_IT_TC) == 0)
-    {
-        HAL_TIM_PWM_Stop_DMA(light->htim, light->Channel);
-        SemaphoreGive(light->Semaphore);
-    }
-}
\ No newline at end of file
```

## 16. Wusong/Port/Src/port_communicate.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Port\Src\port_communicate.c`

模块/函数：接收器 / Rx_Receive。

修改原因：固定帧长、边界检查、坏帧滑动重同步。

影响范围：适配三条14字节链路，防止错误长帧复制越界。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Port/Src/port_communicate.c b/Wusong/Port/Src/port_communicate.c
index f930009..db7fdf5 100644
--- a/Wusong/Port/Src/port_communicate.c
+++ b/Wusong/Port/Src/port_communicate.c
@@ -41,6 +41,9 @@ void Communicate_Tx_Init(Tx_HandleTypeDef *Handle, Tx_InitTypeDef Tx_Init)
 void Rx_Receive(void *self, void *mesg, uint8_t mesg_len)
 {
     Rx_HandleTypeDef *rx = (Rx_HandleTypeDef *)self;
+    /* 接收长度必须落在解包缓冲区内，后续固定按该长度收帧和复制。 */
+    if (mesg == NULL || mesg_len < 2U || mesg_len > sizeof(rx->Queue.Buf))
+        return;
     while (rx->Handle.RingBuf.f_IsEmpty(&rx->Handle.RingBuf) == false)
     {
         /// 从环形缓冲区中读取一个字节，并根据当前状态进行处理
@@ -58,32 +61,34 @@ void Rx_Receive(void *self, void *mesg, uint8_t mesg_len)
                     rx->Queue.Index++;
                 }
                 break;
-            /// 接收数据阶段，直到接收到帧尾或队列满
+            /// 固定14字节帧：数据和CRC中的0xAA/0x55不能提前结束一帧。
             case RECEIVE_DATA:
-                // 若队列未满，继续存储数据；否则放弃当前帧并重同步
-                if (rx->Queue.Index < rx->Queue.Buf_Size)
-                    rx->Queue.Buf[rx->Queue.Index] = rx->CurrData;
-                else
-                    rx->State = WAIT_HEAD;
-                // 若接收到帧尾且帧长度符合条件，进行解包和处理，并重置状态以接收下一帧
-                if (rx->CurrData == rx->Frame_Tail && rx->Queue.Index >= mesg_len - 1)
+                rx->Queue.Buf[rx->Queue.Index++] = rx->CurrData;
+                if (rx->Queue.Index == mesg_len)
                 {
                     rx->State = WAIT_HEAD;
-                    memcpy(mesg, rx->Queue.Buf, rx->Queue.Index + 1);
-                    /// 解包并处理
-                    if (rx->Verify != NULL)
+                    memcpy(mesg, rx->Queue.Buf, mesg_len);
+                    if (rx->CurrData == rx->Frame_Tail &&
+                        (rx->Verify == NULL || rx->Verify(rx, mesg)))
                     {
-                        if (rx->Verify(rx, mesg) == true)
-                            if (rx->Deal != NULL)
-                                rx->Deal(mesg);
+                        if (rx->Deal != NULL)
+                            rx->Deal(mesg);
                     }
                     else
                     {
-                        if (rx->Deal != NULL)
-                            rx->Deal(mesg);
+                        /* 丢字节/坏CRC后保留下一帧可能的帧头。 */
+                        for (uint8_t i = 1U; i < mesg_len; i++)
+                        {
+                            if (rx->Queue.Buf[i] == rx->Frame_Head)
+                            {
+                                rx->Queue.Index = mesg_len - i;
+                                memmove(rx->Queue.Buf, &rx->Queue.Buf[i], rx->Queue.Index);
+                                rx->State = RECEIVE_DATA;
+                                break;
+                            }
+                        }
                     }
                 }
-                rx->Queue.Index++;
                 break;
             }
         }
```

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Port/Src/port_lighteffect.c b/Wusong/Port/Src/port_lighteffect.c
deleted file mode 100644
index 1f30700..0000000
--- a/Wusong/Port/Src/port_lighteffect.c
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,1204 +0,0 @@
-#include "port_lighteffect.h"
-#include "string.h"
-#include "stdlib.h"
-
-Light_t *ColorLightList[MAX_COLORLIGHT_NUM];
-BreathLight_t *BreathLightList[MAX_BREATHLIGHT_NUM];
-NormalLight_t *NormalLightList[MAX_NORMALLIGHT_NUM];
-LightRegister_t LightTable = {0, 0, 0, ColorLightList, BreathLightList, NormalLightList};
-
-// 新增：检查指针是否已在列表中，避免重复注册
-static bool IsRegistered(void *list[], uint8_t num, void *item)
-{
-    // 空指针或数量为0直接返回未注册
-    if (item == NULL || num == 0)
-        return false;
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < num; i++)
-        if (list[i] == item)
-            return true;
-    return false;
-}
-
-/*
- * @brief 灯光注册
- * @param LightType 注册的灯光类型
- * @param light 灯光结构体地址
- */
-uint8_t RegisterLight(LightType_Typedef LightType, void *light)
-{
-    // 空指针保护
-    if (light == NULL)
-        return 1;
-
-    switch (LightType)
-    {
-    case ColorLight:
-        // 避免重复注册与越界
-        if (IsRegistered((void **)LightTable.colorlightlist, LightTable.colorlight_num, light))
-            return 2;
-        if (LightTable.colorlight_num >= MAX_COLORLIGHT_NUM)
-            return 3;
-        LightTable.colorlightlist[LightTable.colorlight_num] = (Light_t *)light;
-        LightTable.colorlightlist[LightTable.colorlight_num]->Init = true;
-        SemaphoreGive(LightTable.colorlightlist[LightTable.colorlight_num]->Semaphore);
-        LightTable.colorlight_num++;
-        return 0;
-    case BreathLight:
-        if (IsRegistered((void **)LightTable.breathlightlist, LightTable.breathlight_num, light))
-            return 2;
-        if (LightTable.breathlight_num >= MAX_BREATHLIGHT_NUM)
-            return 3;
-        LightTable.breathlightlist[LightTable.breathlight_num] = (BreathLight_t *)light;
-        LightTable.breathlight_num++;
-        return 0;
-    case NormalLight:
-        if (IsRegistered((void **)LightTable.normallightlist, LightTable.normallight_num, light))
-            return 2;
-        if (LightTable.normallight_num >= MAX_NORMALLIGHT_NUM)
-            return 3;
-        LightTable.normallightlist[LightTable.normallight_num] = (NormalLight_t *)light;
-        LightTable.normallight_num++;
-        return 0;
-    default:
-        // 未知类型不处理
-        return 4;
-    }
-}
-
-/// @brief 定时器中断服务函数，用于给注册的定时器递增计数
-void LightEffectTimer_ISR(void)
-{
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < LightTable.colorlight_num; i++)
-        LightTable.colorlightlist[i]->TimerCount++;
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < LightTable.breathlight_num; i++)
-        LightTable.breathlightlist[i]->TimerCount++;
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < LightTable.normallight_num; i++)
-        LightTable.normallightlist[i]->TimerCount++;
-}
-
-// 彩灯灯效非阻塞函数
-static int min(uint16_t a, uint16_t b)
-{
-    if (a <= b)
-        return a;
-    else
-        return b;
-}
-void LightEffect_Unblock_SetColor(Light_t *light, uint16_t start, uint16_t end, RGB_t color, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness, bool circle)
-{
-    if (light->Init == true)
-    {
-        light->Init = false;
-        //if (SemaphoreTake(light->Semaphore) == true)
-        {
-            // RGB_SetMoreColor(light, start, end, NONE, AbsoluteLightness, 0);
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(light, start, end, color, AbsoluteLightness, RelativeLightness);
-            RGB_LocalRefresh(light, start, end);
-        }
-    }
-    if (circle == true)
-        light->Init = true;
-}
-void LightEffect_Unblock_Blink(Light_t *light, uint16_t start, uint16_t end, RGB_t color, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness, uint16_t time)
-{
-    if (light->Init == true)
-    {
-        light->Init = false;
-        light->FirstStepCount = 0;
-    }
-    if (light->TimerCount > time && light->FirstStepCount == 0)

-        //if (SemaphoreTake(light->Semaphore) == true)

-            RGB_LocalRefresh(light, start, end);
-            light->FirstStepCount = 1;
-            light->TimerCount = 0;
-        }
-    }
-    if (light->TimerCount > time && light->FirstStepCount == 1)

-        //if (SemaphoreTake(light->Semaphore) == true)

-            RGB_LocalRefresh(light, start, end);
-            light->FirstStepCount = 0;
-            light->TimerCount = 0;
-        }
-    }
-}

-void LightEffect_Unblock_Flow(Light_t *light, uint16_t start, uint16_t end, RGB_t backgroundcolor, RGB_t frontcolor, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness, uint16_t time, uint16_t Holdtime, uint8_t dir)

-void LightEffect_Unblock_Breath(Light_t *light, uint16_t start, uint16_t end, RGB_t color, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint16_t time, uint8_t step, uint8_t (*Function)(uint16_t), uint8_t circle)

-uint8_t Breath_LinearlyIncrease(uint16_t x) // 线性增加
-{
-    int y;
-    if (x > 255)
-        x = 255;
-    y = x;
-    return (uint8_t)y;
-}
-
-uint8_t Breath_LinearlyDiminish(uint16_t x) // 线性减少
-{
-    int y;
-    if (x > 255)
-        x = 255;
-    y = 255 - x;
-    return (uint8_t)y;
-}
-
-uint8_t Breath_QuadraticIncrease(uint16_t x) // 二次函数下凹
-{
-    int y;
-    if (x >= 255)
-        x = 255;
-    y = (2 / 127) * (x - 127) * (x - 127);
-    return (uint8_t)y;
-}
-
-uint8_t Breath_QuadraticDiminish(uint16_t x) // 二次函数上凸
-{
-    int y;
-    if (x >= 255)
-        x = 255;
-    y = 254 - (2 / 127) * (x - 127) * (x - 127);
-    return (uint8_t)y;
-}
-
-uint8_t Breath_LinearlyEpirelief(uint16_t x) // 线性上凸
-{
-    int y;
-    if (x <= 127)
-        y = 2 * x;
-    if (x > 127 && x <= 254)
-        y = 508 - 2 * x;
-    else if (x > 254)
-        y = 0;
-    return (uint8_t)y;
-}
-
-uint8_t Breath_LinearlyFovea(uint16_t x) // 线性下凹
-{
-    int y;
-    if (x <= 127)
-        y = 254 - 2 * x;
-    if (x > 127 && x <= 254)
-        y = 2 * x - 254;
-    else if (x > 254)
-        y = 254;
-    return (uint8_t)y;
-}
-
-void BreathLight_SetLightEffect(BreathLight_t *Light, uint16_t Time, uint16_t Step, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint16_t DelayTime, uint16_t HoldonTime, uint8_t (*Function)(uint16_t), uint8_t Circle)
-{
-    if (Light->Init == true)
-    {
-        Light->Init = false;
-        Light->TimerCount = 0;
-        Light->StepCount = 0;
-        // Light->CCR = 0;
-        //__HAL_TIM_SetCompare(Light->htim, Light->Channel, Light->CCR);
-    }
-    if (Light->TimerCount >= Time + DelayTime) // 若计时时间超过间隔时间
-    {
-        if (Light->StepCount <= 255)
-        {
-            Light->TimerCount = DelayTime;
-            Light->StepCount += Step; // 步进一步
-            Light->CCR = min(Function(Light->StepCount) * Light->ReloadCounter / 255, Light->ReloadCounter);
-            __HAL_TIM_SetCompare(Light->htim, Light->Channel, Light->CCR * AbsoluteLightness / 10);
-        }
-        if (Light->StepCount > 255 && Light->TimerCount >= Time + DelayTime + HoldonTime) // 若步进数超过100且超出保持时间
-        {
-            if (Circle == 0) // 若不是循环则停止步进
-            {
-                Light->StepCount = 255;
-                Light->TimerCount = Time + DelayTime + HoldonTime;
-                return;
-            }
-            else
-            {
-                Light->StepCount = 0;
-                Light->TimerCount = DelayTime;
-            }
-        }
-    }
-}
-
-void BreathLight_SetLightKeep(BreathLight_t *Light, uint16_t DelayTime, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint8_t RelativeLightness)
-{
-    if (Light->Init == true)
-    {
-        Light->TimerCount = 0;
-    }
-    if (Light->TimerCount >= DelayTime && Light->Init == true)

-        Light->Init = false;

-}
-
-void BreathLight_SetBlink(BreathLight_t *light, uint16_t time, uint8_t AbsoluteLightness, uint16_t minLightness, uint16_t maxLightness, uint16_t DelayTime)
```

## 19. Wusong/Task/Inc/CommTask.h

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Task\Inc\CommTask.h`

模块/函数：协议定义 / 方向、命令、Mesg_TypeDef、对外接口。

修改原因：安卓编号恢复参考武松定义；增加从板协议和状态接口。

影响范围：保留14字节布局，去除其他机型的命令含义冲突。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
-#define r_SystemReset 0xF0            // 系统复位/进入Bootloader
-
-/// 接收消息结构体
+/* 微动板命令：通道掩码用Data3/4，阀位置与场景号用Data4。 */
+#define BALL_CMD_VERSION 0x00U
+#define BALL_CMD_CHANNEL_LIGHT 0x01U
+#define BALL_CMD_VALVE 0x02U
+#define BALL_CMD_HOLE_VALVE 0x03U // 补充位0按左右直接推出，补充位1且Data4=0条件成对清洞
+#define BALL_CMD_SCENE 0x07U // Data4=原场景0~11，场景0启用后台微动跟灯
+/* 将原安卓0xFF停止命令延伸到球盘，立即断开三个阀并取消待重发动作。 */
+#define BALL_CMD_STOP 0xFFU
```

影响范围：主控协议与远程球盘业务；接入已有Bootloader串口升级，其他F0数据继续普通复位，备份域不可写时不复位。

```diff
+static void Ball_FlowTask(void)

+            return;

+}
+
+/* 微动板上报只在主循环处理；重传先应答后去重，防止重复计算输赢或清洞。 */
+static void USART2_Deal(void *Rx_mesg)
+{
+    Mesg_TypeDef *mesg = (Mesg_TypeDef *)Rx_mesg;
+    if (mesg->Code1 == Board_to_Ball)
     {
-        /// 重发列表中去除该消息包
-        List_DeleteNode(&ResendList, mesg->ID);
+        if (MesgPort[mesg->ID] == &Tx2 &&
+            memcmp(&MesgTable[mesg->ID].Code1, &mesg->Code1, 8U) == 0)
+            List_DeleteNode(&ResendList, mesg->ID);
+        return;
     }
+    if (mesg->Code1 != Ball_to_Board)
+        return;
+    if (mesg->ACKbyte == 1U)
+        USART_RequestMesg(&Tx2, mesg);
+    if (mesg->ResendID != 0U && List_IsExistID(&BallDealList, mesg->ID))
+        return;
+    switch (mesg->Code2)
+    {
+    case BALL_REPORT_VERSION:
+        BallVersion = ((uint32_t)mesg->Data1 << 24) | ((uint32_t)mesg->Data2 << 16) |
+                      ((uint32_t)mesg->Data3 << 8) | mesg->Data4;
+        break;
+    case BALL_REPORT_SWITCH:
+        if (mesg->Data4 < 12U && mesg->ExpandCode <= 1U)
+        {
+            if (Scene == SCENE_PLAYING || Scene == SCENE_ADDITIONAL)
+            {
+                /* 微动板锁存闭合瞬间的灯态，补充位0=未中奖、1=中奖。 */
+                CommTransmitFillData(&Tx1, Board_to_Android,
+                    mesg->ExpandCode == 1U ? t_Win : t_Lose, 0U, 0U);
+                CommTransmitFillData(&Tx1, Board_to_Android, t_Switch, mesg->Data4, 0U);
+            }
+        }
+        break;
+    case BALL_REPORT_HOLE:
+        if (mesg->Data4 <= 1U)
+        {
+            CommTransmitFillData(&Tx1, Board_to_Android, t_Hole, mesg->Data4, 0U);
+            if (Scene != SCENE_ADDITIONAL && Scene != SCENE_PLAYING &&
+                Scene != SCENE_HAPPY1 && Scene != SCENE_HAPPY2 && Scene != SCENE_SETTING)
+            {
+                Ball_RequestEjectIfOccupied();
+            }
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    if (List_IsExistID(&BallDealList, mesg->ID))
+        List_DeleteNode(&BallDealList, mesg->ID);
+    List_AddNode(&BallDealList, mesg->ID, HAL_GetTick());
 }

+/* 兼容原武松上报帧的ACK位，功能码和Data/Expand布局保持不变。 */
+void CommTransmitFillData(Tx_HandleTypeDef *Tx, uint8_t code_1, uint8_t code_2, uint32_t data, uint8_t expandCode)
+{
+    switch (code_2)
+    {
+    case t_HoolleInput: case t_CoinInput: case t_HoolleOutputTimeOut: case t_CardOutputTimeOut:
+    case t_Switch: case t_Hole: case t_HoolleFinish: case t_CardOnce: case t_CardFinish:
+    case t_Win: case t_Lose: case t_AntiShake:
+        Comm_SendMesg_FillData_withResend(Tx, code_1, code_2, data, expandCode, &ResendList);
+        break;
+    default:
+        Comm_SendMesg_FillData(Tx, code_1, code_2, data, expandCode);
+        break;
+    }
+}
```

## 26. Wusong/Task/Src/CtrlTask.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Task\Src\CtrlTask.c`

模块/函数：机构任务 / Card_Output、CtrlTask。

修改原因：恢复零数量出卡完成通知，电子锁保持原2000ms；原扭蛋通道改为第二路出珠并暂停调度，保持初始化断电。清理错误机型和0x21协议注释。

影响范围：第一路出珠、吐卡、开锁及舵机初始化保持；第二路未接入安卓启动命令。

```diff
+
+/* 丢帧或串口错误后复位当前解包状态并重新挂接接收，避免链路永久停收。 */
+void HAL_UART_ErrorCallback(UART_HandleTypeDef *huart)
+{
+    Rx_HandleTypeDef *rx = NULL;
+    if (huart == Rx1.Handle.huart) rx = &Rx1;
+    else if (huart == Rx2.Handle.huart) rx = &Rx2;
+    else if (huart == Rx3.Handle.huart) rx = &Rx3;
+    if (rx != NULL)
+    {
+        HAL_UART_AbortReceive(huart);
+        rx->State = WAIT_HEAD;
+        rx->Queue.Index = 0U;
+        HAL_UART_Receive_IT(huart, &rx->Handle.temp_data, 1U);
+    }
+}
```

```diff
-        init.longpress_time = KEY_LONG_PRESS_TIME;
+        /* 原武松K2长按阈值4秒，其余后台按键为1.5秒。 */
+        init.longpress_time = (i == 1U) ? 4000U : 1500U;
         init.trigger_frequnecy = 1;
         init.trigger_level = GPIO_PIN_RESET;
         init.key_id = i;
```

```diff
-void PinkLight_SetState(uint8_t light_id, uint8_t state)

-        refresh_pending = &PinkRightRefreshPending;
-    }
-    else
-    {
-        return;
-    }
-
-    /* 中文注释：粉灯不支持流水0x02，只处理关闭、打开和闪烁 */
-    if (state != LIGHT_STATE_OFF &&
-        state != LIGHT_STATE_ON &&
-        state != LIGHT_STATE_BLINK)
-    {
-        return;
-    }
-
-    light->state = state;
-
-    if (state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK)

-        /* 中文注释：每只眼收到闪烁命令后独立从亮250ms开始计时 */

-        *blink_tick = HAL_GetTick();

-
-    *refresh_pending = 1U;
-}

-static void PinkLightBufferFlush(Light_Handle_t *light, uint8_t blink_on)
-{

-        (light->state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK && blink_on != 0U))
-    {
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, PINK, *(light->Lightness), 255);
-    }
-    else
-    {
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, NONE, *(light->Lightness), 0);
-    }
-}
-
-static void PinkLight_Task(void)
-{
-    uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
-    uint8_t need_flush = 0U;
-
-    /* 中文注释：左眼闪烁只更新左眼自己的相位和刷新标志 */
-    if (Light_PLeft.state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK &&
-        (uint32_t)(now - PinkLeftBlinkTick) >= LIGHT_BLINK_HALF_TIME)
-    {
-        PinkLeftBlinkTick = now;
-        PinkLeftBlinkOn = (PinkLeftBlinkOn == 0U) ? 1U : 0U;
-        PinkLeftRefreshPending = 1U;
-    }
-
-    /* 中文注释：右眼闪烁只更新右眼自己的相位和刷新标志 */
-    if (Light_PRight.state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK &&
-        (uint32_t)(now - PinkRightBlinkTick) >= LIGHT_BLINK_HALF_TIME)
-    {
-        PinkRightBlinkTick = now;
-        PinkRightBlinkOn = (PinkRightBlinkOn == 0U) ? 1U : 0U;
-        PinkRightRefreshPending = 1U;
-    }
-
-    if (PinkLeftRefreshPending != 0U)
-    {
-        PinkLightBufferFlush(&Light_PLeft, PinkLeftBlinkOn);
-        PinkLeftRefreshPending = 0U;
-        need_flush = 1U;
-    }
-
-    if (PinkRightRefreshPending != 0U)
-    {
-        PinkLightBufferFlush(&Light_PRight, PinkRightBlinkOn);
-        PinkRightRefreshPending = 0U;
-        need_flush = 1U;
-    }
-
-    if (need_flush != 0U)
-    {
-        /* 中文注释：鼻子32~35始终关闭；左右眼颜色只由各自协议状态决定 */
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, PINK_NOSE_START, PINK_NOSE_END, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 0);
-        RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-    }
-}
-
-static void IdleLight1Flow_Task(void)
-{
-    uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
-
-    if (IdleLight1FlowActive == 0U)
-    {
-        IdleLight1FlowActive = 1U;
-        IdleLight1FlowHold = 0U;
-        IdleLight1FlowIndex = 0U;
-        IdleLight1FlowTick = now;
-        IdleLight1FlowHoldTick = now;
-
-        /* 中文注释：待机流水只清蓝灯区域，跳过鼻子和左右眼 */
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 0, 31, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 0);
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 36, 61, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 0);
-        RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-        return;
-    }
-
-    if (IdleLight1FlowHold != 0U)
-    {
-        if ((uint32_t)(now - IdleLight1FlowHoldTick) >= LIGHT1_IDLE_FLOW_HOLD_TIME)
-        {
-            IdleLight1FlowHold = 0U;
-            IdleLight1FlowIndex = 0U;
-            IdleLight1FlowTick = now;
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 0, 31, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 0);
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 36, 61, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 0);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-        }
-        return;
-    }
-
-    if ((uint32_t)(now - IdleLight1FlowTick) < LIGHT1_IDLE_FLOW_STEP_TIME)
-        return;
-
-    IdleLight1FlowTick = now;
-
-    /* 中文注释：仍按原66个灯位计时，仅在粉灯位置跳过写入，保持原流水节奏 */
-    if (IdleLight1FlowIndex <= 31U ||
-        (IdleLight1FlowIndex >= 36U && IdleLight1FlowIndex <= 61U))
-    {
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, IdleLight1FlowIndex, IdleLight1FlowIndex, SKYBLUE, LightBoard_Lightness, 255);
-        RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-    }
-
-    IdleLight1FlowIndex++;
-    if (IdleLight1FlowIndex >= LIGHT1_IDLE_FLOW_COUNT)
-    {
-        IdleLight1FlowHold = 1U;
-        IdleLight1FlowHoldTick = now;
-    }
-}
-
-/// 洞口灯光流水/开关/闪烁任务
-static void LightBufferFlush(Light_Handle_t *light, RGB_t color)
-{
-    if (light->state == LIGHT_STATE_FLOW)
-    {
-        light->index++;
-        if (light->index > light->end)
-        {
-            light->index = light->start;
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, NONE, *(light->Lightness), 255);
-        }
-        else
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->index, color, *(light->Lightness), 255);
-    }
-    else if (light->state == LIGHT_STATE_OFF)
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, NONE, *(light->Lightness), 0);
-    else if (light->state == LIGHT_STATE_ON)
-        RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, color, *(light->Lightness), 255);
-    else if (light->state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK)
-    {
-        /* 中文注释：蓝灯、黄灯0x03为500ms周期闪烁 */
-        if (LightBlinkOn != 0U)
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, color, *(light->Lightness), 255);
-        else
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(light->light, light->start, light->end, NONE, *(light->Lightness), 0);
-    }
-}
-
-static void LightBlink_Task(void)
-{
-    uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
-    uint8_t has_blink = 0U;
-    uint8_t refresh_light1 = 0U;
-    uint8_t refresh_light2 = 0U;
-
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++)
-    {
-        if (Light_BLUE[i]->state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK ||
-            Light_YELLOW[i]->state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK)
-        {
-            has_blink = 1U;
-            break;
-        }
-    }
-
-    if (has_blink == 0U)
-    {
-        LightBlinkOn = 1U;
-        LightBlinkTick = now;
-        return;
-    }
-
-    if ((uint32_t)(now - LightBlinkTick) < LIGHT_BLINK_HALF_TIME)
-        return;
-
-    LightBlinkTick = now;
-    LightBlinkOn = (LightBlinkOn == 0U) ? 1U : 0U;
-
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++)
-    {
-        if (Light_BLUE[i]->state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK)
-        {
-            LightBufferFlush(Light_BLUE[i], SKYBLUE);
-            refresh_light1 = 1U;
-        }
-        if (Light_YELLOW[i]->state == LIGHT_STATE_BLINK)
-        {
-            LightBufferFlush(Light_YELLOW[i], GREEN);
-            refresh_light2 = 1U;
-        }
-    }
-
-    if (refresh_light1 != 0U)
-        RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-    if (refresh_light2 != 0U)
-        RGB_Flush(&Light2);
-}
-
-/// 洞口灯光刷新任务
-static void LightFlush_Task(void)
-{
-    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; i++)
-    {
-        LightBufferFlush(Light_BLUE[i], SKYBLUE);
-        LightBufferFlush(Light_YELLOW[i], GREEN);
-    }
-    RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-    RGB_Flush(&Light2);
-}
-
-/// 灯光任务
-void LightTask(void)
-{
-    if (Scene != IdleScene)
-        IdleLight1FlowActive = 0U;
-
-    if (Scene != PlayingScene)
-    {
-        LightBlinkOn = 1U;
-        LightBlinkTick = HAL_GetTick();
-    }
-
-    if (Scene == SettingScene)
-    {
-        /* 中文注释：设置场景只点亮蓝灯区域，跳过鼻子和左右眼 */
-        LightEffect_Unblock_SetColor(&Light1, 0, 31, WHITE, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, true);
-        LightEffect_Unblock_SetColor(&Light1, 36, 61, WHITE, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, true);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J2, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J3, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J6, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J7, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        LightEffect_Unblock_SetColor(&Light2, 0, Light2_RGBbufSize, WHITE, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, true);
-    }
-    if (Scene == IdleScene)
-    {
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J2, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J3, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J6, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J7, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        /* 中文注释：Light1待机流水跳过粉灯区域，Light2保持原黄灯流水 */
-        IdleLight1Flow_Task();
-        LightEffect_Unblock_Flow(&Light2, 0, Light2_RGBbufSize, NONE, GREEN, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, 50, 1000, 0);
-    }
-    if (Scene == PlayingScene)
-    {
-        static uint32_t time = 0;
-        if (EventGroupCheckBits(&Event, Event_SceneChange) == true)
-        {
-            /* 中文注释：场景切换只清0~61，禁止改写左右眼62~65 */
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 0, 61, NONE, 0, 0);
-            RGB_SetAllColor(&Light2, NONE, 0, 0);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light2);
-            //     // LightResume();
-            EventGroupClearBits(&Event, Event_SceneChange);
-        }
-        LightBlink_Task();
-        if (HAL_GetTick() - time > 100)
-        {
-            LightFlush_Task();
-            time = HAL_GetTick();
-        }
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J2, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J3, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J6, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        BreathLight_SetLightKeep(&J7, 0, Setting.LightBelt_Lightness, 255);
-        // LightEffect_Unblock_SetColor(&Light1, 0, Light1_RGBbufSize, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, 0);
-        // LightEffect_Unblock_SetColor(&Light2, 0, Light2_RGBbufSize, NONE, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, 0);
-        // LightEffect_Unblock_Blink(&Light1, 36, 37, PINK, LightBoard_Lightness, 255, 250);
-    }
-    if (Scene == LittleGame_1)
-    {
-        if (EventGroupCheckBits(&Event, Event_SceneChange))
-        {
-            EventGroupClearBits(&Event, Event_SceneChange);
-            /* 中文注释：小游戏场景只清0~61，禁止改写左右眼62~65 */
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 0, 61, NONE, 0, 0);
-            RGB_CleanAll(&Light2);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light2);
-        }
-    }
-    if (Scene == LittleGame_2)
-    {
-        if (EventGroupCheckBits(&Event, Event_SceneChange))
-        {
-            EventGroupClearBits(&Event, Event_SceneChange);
-            /* 中文注释：小游戏场景只清0~61，禁止改写左右眼62~65 */
-            RGB_SetMoreColor(&Light1, 0, 61, NONE, 0, 0);
-            RGB_CleanAll(&Light2);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light1);
-            RGB_Flush(&Light2);
-        }
-    }
-
-    /* 中文注释：左右眼只根据通信设置的状态及其闪烁时基运行 */

-}
\ No newline at end of file
```

## 30. Wusong/Task/Src/MainTask.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong\Task\Src\MainTask.c`

模块/函数：主任务 / Main_Init。

修改原因：发送控台/球盘版本查询并同步初始场景，取消不存在的RGB控台命令。

影响范围：上电初始化与实际从板协议匹配。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong/Task/Src/MainTask.c b/Wusong/Task/Src/MainTask.c
index 5d3cc92..fdb63f9 100644
--- a/Wusong/Task/Src/MainTask.c
+++ b/Wusong/Task/Src/MainTask.c
@@ -11,11 +11,12 @@

 #define SYSLIGHT_BLINK_TIME 500

-/* 兼容旧工程中仍参与编译但已退出运行路径的灯效模块，第二阶段删除对应工程项后再移除。 */
+/* 保留模板枚举供原工程编译，实际游戏场景按原武松SCENE_*编号处理。 */
 Scene_t Scene = IdleScene;
 Event_Handle_t Event;

 extern Tx_HandleTypeDef Tx3;
+extern Tx_HandleTypeDef Tx2;

 void System_Reset(void)
 {
@@ -44,31 +45,11 @@ void Main_Init(void)
     /* 中文注释：主板本地数码管继续保留，控台数码管通过USART3同步接收显示数据。 */
     DigitalTubeTask_Init();

-    /*
-     * 控台上电初始化沿用小丑协议：
```

## 36. Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/gpio.c

模块/函数：GPIO / MX_GPIO_Init。

修改原因：配置12路低有效微动、两洞口、SM16306和三阀；关闭JTAG保留SWD。

影响范围：完全依据微动板引脚；阀上电低电平。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff

--- a/Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/gpio.c
+++ b/Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/gpio.c

 void MX_GPIO_Init(void)
 {
   GPIO_InitTypeDef GPIO_InitStruct = {0};
-
-  __HAL_RCC_GPIOC_CLK_ENABLE();
-  __HAL_RCC_GPIOD_CLK_ENABLE();
   __HAL_RCC_GPIOA_CLK_ENABLE();
   __HAL_RCC_GPIOB_CLK_ENABLE();
   __HAL_RCC_AFIO_CLK_ENABLE();
-
-  /* 中文注释：PB3/PB4作为球盘光眼输入，PA15作为状态灯GPIO，关闭JTAG并保留SWD。 */
+  /* PA15/PB3/PB4是微动输入，关闭JTAG，保留PA13/PA14的SWD。 */
   __HAL_AFIO_REMAP_SWJ_NOJTAG();

-  /* 中文注释：User_Led由3.3V经电阻接到PA15，低电平点亮。 */
-  // HAL_GPIO_WritePin(User_Led_GPIO_Port, User_Led_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);

-  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(BallLight_GPIO_Port, BallLight_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
-  /* 中文注释：SEG_OE经两级反相后与OE#同极性，PB12低电平使能显示，高电平关闭显示。 */
-  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(Tube_OE_GPIO_Port, Tube_OE_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
-  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(Tube_RCLK_GPIO_Port, Tube_RCLK_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
-  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(Tube_SRCLK_GPIO_Port, Tube_SRCLK_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
-  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(Tube_SER_GPIO_Port, Tube_SER_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
-
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = User_Led_Pin;
+  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Valve_Left_Pin | Valve_Right_Pin | Valve_Block_Pin |
+                    Channel_SDI_Pin | Channel_CLK_Pin | Channel_LE_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
+  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = Valve_Left_Pin | Valve_Right_Pin | Valve_Block_Pin |
+                        Channel_SDI_Pin | Channel_CLK_Pin | Channel_LE_Pin;
   GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT_PP;
   GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_LOW;
-  // HAL_GPIO_Init(User_Led_GPIO_Port, &GPIO_InitStruct);
-
-  /* 中文注释：PB3~PB7和PA4/PA5均由原理图外围电阻提供电平，MCU不额外上下拉。 */
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = BallEye1_Pin | BallEye2_Pin | BallEye3_Pin | BallEye4_Pin | BallEye5_Pin;
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
+  GPIO_InitStruct.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_HIGH;
   HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOB, &GPIO_InitStruct);

-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = BallEyeFB1_Pin | BallEyeFB2_Pin;
+  HAL_GPIO_WritePin(User_Led_GPIO_Port, User_Led_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
+  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = User_Led_Pin;

+  HAL_GPIO_Init(User_Led_GPIO_Port, &GPIO_InitStruct);
+

+                        GPIO_PIN_7 | GPIO_PIN_8 | GPIO_PIN_11 | GPIO_PIN_12 | GPIO_PIN_15;
   GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
   GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
   HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOA, &GPIO_InitStruct);

-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = Tube_OE_Pin | Tube_RCLK_Pin | Tube_SRCLK_Pin | Tube_SER_Pin;
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT_PP;
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
-  GPIO_InitStruct.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_HIGH;
+  GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = GPIO_PIN_3 | GPIO_PIN_4 | GPIO_PIN_5 | Hole_Left_Pin | Hole_Right_Pin;
```

## 39. Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/usart.c

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/usart.c b/Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/usart.c

--- a/Wusong_Ball/Core/Src/usart.c

 /* USER CODE END Header */

+void MX_USART1_UART_Init(void)
 {

+  huart1.Instance = USART1;
+  huart1.Init.BaudRate = 115200;
+  huart1.Init.WordLength = UART_WORDLENGTH_8B;
+  huart1.Init.StopBits = UART_STOPBITS_1;
+  huart1.Init.Parity = UART_PARITY_NONE;
+  huart1.Init.Mode = UART_MODE_TX_RX;
+  huart1.Init.HwFlowCtl = UART_HWCONTROL_NONE;
+  huart1.Init.OverSampling = UART_OVERSAMPLING_16;

   {
     Error_Handler();
   }
@@ -32,33 +32,33 @@ void HAL_UART_MspInit(UART_HandleTypeDef* uartHandle)
 {
   GPIO_InitTypeDef GPIO_InitStruct = {0};

     GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_AF_PP;
     GPIO_InitStruct.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_HIGH;
     HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOA, &GPIO_InitStruct);

-    GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = GPIO_PIN_3;
+    GPIO_InitStruct.Pin = GPIO_PIN_10;
     GPIO_InitStruct.Mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
     GPIO_InitStruct.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
     HAL_GPIO_Init(GPIOA, &GPIO_InitStruct);

-    /* 中文注释：原模板遗漏USART2中断，球盘协议接收依赖该中断。 */

+    HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(USART1_IRQn, 0, 0);
+    HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(USART1_IRQn);
   }
 }

 void HAL_UART_MspDeInit(UART_HandleTypeDef* uartHandle)
 {

 }
```

## 40. Wusong_Ball/Lib/Src/app_ringbuf.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong_Ball\Lib\Src\app_ringbuf.c`

模块/函数：串口环形缓冲 / RingBuffer_IsEmpty/IsFull/GetSpace/GetDatalen/ReadByte。

修改原因：满缓冲时索引相同却被判空，四字节容量测试已复现；改用Count判空判满，读操作短临界区与现有主控一致。

影响范围：微动板串口突发接收；不改变缓冲结构或通信协议。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong_Ball/Lib/Src/app_ringbuf.c b/Wusong_Ball/Lib/Src/app_ringbuf.c
index 2de6569..0880462 100644
--- a/Wusong_Ball/Lib/Src/app_ringbuf.c
+++ b/Wusong_Ball/Lib/Src/app_ringbuf.c
@@ -11,10 +11,7 @@ static bool RingBuffer_IsEmpty(void *RB)
     RingBuffer_t *rb = (RingBuffer_t *)RB;
     if (rb == NULL)
         return true;
-    if (rb->Write_index >= rb->Read_index)
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index - rb->Read_index;
-    else
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index + rb->Size - rb->Read_index;
+    /* 索引重合既可能为空也可能为满，必须使用实际字节计数区分。 */
     return (rb->Count == 0);
 }

@@ -28,10 +25,6 @@ static bool RingBuffer_IsFull(void *RB)
     RingBuffer_t *rb = (RingBuffer_t *)RB;
     if (rb == NULL)
         return true;
-    if (rb->Write_index >= rb->Read_index)
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index - rb->Read_index;
-    else
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index + rb->Size - rb->Read_index;
     return (rb->Count == rb->Size);
 }

@@ -43,10 +36,6 @@ static bool RingBuffer_IsFull(void *RB)
 static uint16_t RingBuffer_GetSpace(void *RB)
 {
     RingBuffer_t *rb = (RingBuffer_t *)RB;
-    if (rb->Write_index >= rb->Read_index)
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index - rb->Read_index;
-    else
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index + rb->Size - rb->Read_index;
     return rb->Size - rb->Count;
 }

@@ -60,10 +49,6 @@ static uint16_t RingBuffer_GetDatalen(void *RB)
     RingBuffer_t *rb = (RingBuffer_t *)RB;
     if (rb == NULL)
         return 0;
-    if (rb->Write_index >= rb->Read_index)
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index - rb->Read_index;
-    else
-        rb->Count = rb->Write_index + rb->Size - rb->Read_index;
     return rb->Count;
 }

@@ -99,13 +84,33 @@ static bool RingBuffer_WriteByte(void *RB, uint8_t data)
 static bool RingBuffer_ReadByte(void *RB, uint8_t *data)
 {
     RingBuffer_t *rb = (RingBuffer_t *)RB;
+    uint32_t primask;
+
     if (rb == NULL || data == NULL)
         return false;
-    if (RingBuffer_IsEmpty(rb))
+
+    /*
+     * RingBuffer_WriteByte() 会在 UART 接收中断中修改
+     * Write_index 和 Count。
+     *
+     * 读取端必须保证“判空、读取、更新 Read_index、Count--”
+     * 是一个不可被 UART 中断打断的完整操作。
+     */
+    primask = __get_PRIMASK();
+    __disable_irq();
+
+    if (rb->Count == 0)
+    {
+        __set_PRIMASK(primask);
         return false;
+    }
+
     *data = rb->Buffer[rb->Read_index];
-    rb->Read_index = (rb->Read_index + 1) % rb->Size;
+    rb->Read_index = (rb->Read_index + 1U) % rb->Size;
     rb->Count--;
+
+    __set_PRIMASK(primask);
+
     return true;
 }
```

## 42. Wusong_Ball/Mid/Src/port_communicate.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong_Ball\Mid\Src\port_communicate.c`

模块/函数：接收器 / Rx_Receive。

修改原因：固定帧长、边界检查与坏帧滑动重同步。

影响范围：与主控一致的14字节接收器；控台小卡文件未修改。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong_Ball/Mid/Src/port_communicate.c b/Wusong_Ball/Mid/Src/port_communicate.c
index 7c7ca70..db7fdf5 100644
--- a/Wusong_Ball/Mid/Src/port_communicate.c
+++ b/Wusong_Ball/Mid/Src/port_communicate.c
@@ -41,6 +41,9 @@ void Communicate_Tx_Init(Tx_HandleTypeDef *Handle, Tx_InitTypeDef Tx_Init)
 void Rx_Receive(void *self, void *mesg, uint8_t mesg_len)
 {
     Rx_HandleTypeDef *rx = (Rx_HandleTypeDef *)self;
+    /* 接收长度必须落在解包缓冲区内，后续固定按该长度收帧和复制。 */
+    if (mesg == NULL || mesg_len < 2U || mesg_len > sizeof(rx->Queue.Buf))
+        return;
     while (rx->Handle.RingBuf.f_IsEmpty(&rx->Handle.RingBuf) == false)
     {
         /// 从环形缓冲区中读取一个字节，并根据当前状态进行处理
@@ -58,36 +61,34 @@ void Rx_Receive(void *self, void *mesg, uint8_t mesg_len)
                     rx->Queue.Index++;
                 }
                 break;
-            /// 接收数据阶段，收满固定帧长后再检查帧尾
+            /// 固定14字节帧：数据和CRC中的0xAA/0x55不能提前结束一帧。
             case RECEIVE_DATA:
-                // 若队列未满，继续存储数据；否则放弃当前帧并重同步
-                if (rx->Queue.Index < rx->Queue.Buf_Size)
-                    rx->Queue.Buf[rx->Queue.Index] = rx->CurrData;
-                else
-                    rx->State = WAIT_HEAD;
-                // 协议固定14字节，ID/数据/CRC中的0x55是合法内容，不能提前结束接收。
-                // 仅在第14字节检查帧尾，错误帧丢弃；拷贝长度仍固定，避免长帧越界。
-                if (rx->Queue.Index == mesg_len - 1)
+                rx->Queue.Buf[rx->Queue.Index++] = rx->CurrData;
+                if (rx->Queue.Index == mesg_len)
                 {
                     rx->State = WAIT_HEAD;
-                    if (rx->CurrData == rx->Frame_Tail)
+                    memcpy(mesg, rx->Queue.Buf, mesg_len);
+                    if (rx->CurrData == rx->Frame_Tail &&
+                        (rx->Verify == NULL || rx->Verify(rx, mesg)))
                     {
-                        memcpy(mesg, rx->Queue.Buf, mesg_len);
-                        /// 解包并处理
-                        if (rx->Verify != NULL)
-                        {
-                            if (rx->Verify(rx, mesg) == true)
-                                if (rx->Deal != NULL)
-                                    rx->Deal(mesg);
-                        }
-                        else
+                        if (rx->Deal != NULL)
+                            rx->Deal(mesg);
+                    }
+                    else
+                    {
+                        /* 丢字节/坏CRC后保留下一帧可能的帧头。 */
+                        for (uint8_t i = 1U; i < mesg_len; i++)
                         {
-                            if (rx->Deal != NULL)
-                                rx->Deal(mesg);
+                            if (rx->Queue.Buf[i] == rx->Frame_Head)
+                            {
+                                rx->Queue.Index = mesg_len - i;
+                                memmove(rx->Queue.Buf, &rx->Queue.Buf[i], rx->Queue.Index);
+                                rx->State = RECEIVE_DATA;
+                                break;
+                            }
                         }
                     }
                 }
-                rx->Queue.Index++;
                 break;
             }
         }
```

## 45. Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/CommTask.c

模块/函数：通信/阀 / USART_Deal、USART_SendMesg、Comm_SendMesg_FillData、CommInit/Task、BallValve_Start/Task/EjectIfOccupied。

修改原因：处理主控命令、原方向回显应答、重发去重、本地阀时序；补充位1条件清洞保留任一洞占用且两阀空闲的原判断；同步场景并隔离后台测试灯态和游戏掩码。

影响范围：挡珠800ms；左右洞沿用原TriggerCount=1的800ms/300ms/800ms节奏。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/CommTask.c b/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/CommTask.c
index 2915770..1415414 100644
--- a/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/CommTask.c
+++ b/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/CommTask.c
@@ -1,9 +1,9 @@
 #include "CommTask.h"
-#include "DigitalTubeTask.h"
 #include "LightTask.h"
 #include "app_crc.h"
 #include "string.h"
 #include "usart.h"
+#include "app_list.h"

 #define Mesg_Head 0xAA
 #define Mesg_Tail 0x55
@@ -13,6 +13,73 @@ static Mesg_TypeDef Receive_mesg;

 Tx_HandleTypeDef Tx;
 Rx_HandleTypeDef Rx;
+uint8_t BallScene = BALL_SCENE_IDLE;
+static uint16_t GameChannelMask = 0U; // 保存主控游戏掩码，后台测试灯态不覆盖此值
+
+#define RESEND_MS 1000U
+#define DEAL_KEEP_MS 4500U
+#define VALVE_PULSE_MS 800U
+
+static Mesg_TypeDef Pending[256];
+static ListHandle_t ResendList, DealList;
+static ListNode_t ResendPool[32], DealPool[32];
+static const uint16_t ValvePin[3] = {Valve_Left_Pin, Valve_Right_Pin, Valve_Block_Pin};
+static uint32_t ValveTick[3];
+static uint8_t ValveActive[3]; // 下标依次为左、右、挡珠；0空闲、1吸合、2脉冲间隔
+static uint8_t ValveRepeat[3];
+
+/* 应答直接回显已校验的原帧，保持原方向、ID、ACK位及CRC。 */
+static void USART_Echo(Mesg_TypeDef *mesg)
+{
+    HAL_UART_Transmit(Tx.huart, (uint8_t *)mesg, sizeof(*mesg), 100U);
+}
+
+/* 本板自行关断电磁阀，不依赖主控再次下发关闭命令。 */
+void BallValve_Task(void)
+{
+    uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
+    for (uint8_t i = 0U; i < 3U; i++)
+    {
+        if (ValveActive[i] == 1U && (uint32_t)(now - ValveTick[i]) >= VALVE_PULSE_MS)
+        {
+            HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, ValvePin[i], GPIO_PIN_RESET);
+            ValveActive[i] = ValveRepeat[i] != 0U ? 2U : 0U;
+            ValveTick[i] = now;
+        }
+        else if (ValveActive[i] == 2U && (uint32_t)(now - ValveTick[i]) >= 300U)
+        {
+            ValveRepeat[i]--;
+            ValveTick[i] = now;
+            ValveActive[i] = 1U;
+            HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, ValvePin[i], GPIO_PIN_SET);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void BallValve_Start(uint8_t index)
+{
+    /* 忙时不延长吸合时间，断线/重传也由球盘本地800ms关断。 */
+    if (index < 3U && ValveActive[index] == 0U)
+    {
+        ValveTick[index] = HAL_GetTick();
+        ValveActive[index] = 1U;
+        /* 左右洞保留原TriggerCount=1的追加一次脉冲，挡珠阀只执行一次。 */
+        ValveRepeat[index] = index < 2U ? 1U : 0U;
+        HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, ValvePin[index], GPIO_PIN_SET);
+    }
+}
+
+static void BallValve_EjectIfOccupied(void)
+{
+    /* 原武松任一洞有球、且两洞阀均空闲时，才成对执行推出。 */
+    if ((HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(GPIOB, Hole_Left_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET ||
+         HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(GPIOB, Hole_Right_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) &&
+        ValveActive[0] == 0U && ValveActive[1] == 0U)
+    {
+        BallValve_Start(0U);
+        BallValve_Start(1U);
+    }
+}

 {
-    Rx.Receive(&Rx, &Receive_mesg, 14);

+    uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
+    Rx.Receive(&Rx, &Receive_mesg, 14U);
+    /* 保持原ID重发，递增重传次数并重算CRC，最多补发三次。 */
+    node = ResendList.Head;
+    while (node != NULL)
+    {
+        ListNode_t *next = node->Next;
+        if ((uint32_t)(now - node->Value) >= RESEND_MS)
+        {
+            uint8_t frame[14];
+            uint16_t crc;
+            Pending[node->ID].ResendID++;
+            memcpy(frame, &Pending[node->ID], sizeof(frame));
+            crc = CRC16_calculate(frame, 11U);
+            frame[11] = (uint8_t)(crc >> 8);
+            frame[12] = (uint8_t)crc;
+            HAL_UART_Transmit(Tx.huart, frame, sizeof(frame), 100U);
+            node->Value = now;
+            if (Pending[node->ID].ResendID >= 3U)
+                List_DeleteNode(&ResendList, node->ID);
+        }
+        node = next;
+    }
+    node = DealList.Head;
+    while (node != NULL)
+    {
+        ListNode_t *next = node->Next;
+        if ((uint32_t)(now - node->Value) >= DEAL_KEEP_MS)
+            List_DeleteNode(&DealList, node->ID);
+        node = next;
+    }
 }
```

## 46. Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/InterruptTask.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong_Ball\Task\Src\InterruptTask.c`

模块/函数：UART回调 / HAL_UART_RxCpltCallback、HAL_UART_ErrorCallback。

修改原因：注释及错误恢复对应USART1。

影响范围：字节入环形缓冲和故障后继续接收。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/InterruptTask.c b/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/InterruptTask.c
index 692cfe7..c5223a1 100644
--- a/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/InterruptTask.c
+++ b/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/InterruptTask.c
@@ -7,8 +7,20 @@ void HAL_UART_RxCpltCallback(UART_HandleTypeDef *huart)
 {
     if (huart == Rx.Handle.huart)
     {
-        /* 中文注释：USART2每收到1字节写入环形缓冲区，并立即继续接收下一字节。 */
+        /* 中文注释：USART1每收到1字节写入环形缓冲区，并立即继续接收下一字节。 */
         Rx.Handle.RingBuf.f_WriteByte(&Rx.Handle.RingBuf, Rx.Handle.temp_data);
         HAL_UART_Receive_IT(huart, &Rx.Handle.temp_data, 1);
     }
 }
+
+/* 错误后丢弃当前未完成帧并重新挂接单字节接收，等待下一帧头重同步。 */
+void HAL_UART_ErrorCallback(UART_HandleTypeDef *huart)
+{
+    if (huart == Rx.Handle.huart)
+    {
+        HAL_UART_AbortReceive(huart);
+        Rx.State = WAIT_HEAD;
+        Rx.Queue.Index = 0U;
+        HAL_UART_Receive_IT(huart, &Rx.Handle.temp_data, 1U);
+    }
+}
```

```diff
-    }
-
-    if (mode > BALL_MODE_BREATH)
-    {
-        return;
-    }
-
-    BallLedColor[led_id - 1U] = color;
-    BallLedMode[led_id - 1U] = mode;
-    BallLightDirty = 1U;
-}
-
-void BallLight_SetBrightness(uint8_t brightness)
+uint16_t BallLight_GetChannels(void)
 {
-    if (brightness > 10U)
-    {
-        brightness = 10U;
-    }
-
-    BallLightness = brightness;
-    BallLightDirty = 1U;
+    return ChannelMask;
 }

-static uint8_t BallLight_GetRelativeLightness(uint8_t mode, uint32_t tick)
+/* SM16306：OE#硬接地，PB15=SDI、PB14=CLK、PB13=LE，无反相器。 */
+void BallLight_SetChannels(uint16_t mask)
 {
-    uint32_t phase;
+    uint16_t output = 0U;
+    ChannelMask = mask & 0x0FFFU;
+    /* 通道bit0(J1)对应LED12/OUT11；bit11(J12)对应LED1/OUT0。 */
+    for (uint8_t i = 0U; i < 12U; i++)
+        if ((mask & (1U << i)) != 0U)
+            output |= (uint16_t)(1U << (11U - i));

-    switch (mode)
+    HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_LE_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
+    /* 先发送高位，完整移入16位后再脉冲锁存，未使用的高四路始终为0。 */
+    for (uint16_t bit = 0x8000U; bit != 0U; bit >>= 1U)
     {
-        case BALL_MODE_ON:
-            return 255U;
-
-        case BALL_MODE_OFF:
-            return 0U;
-
-        case BALL_MODE_BLINK:
-            return ((tick / BALL_LIGHT_BLINK_INTERVAL) % 2U) == 0U ? 255U : 0U;
-
-        case BALL_MODE_BREATH:
-            phase = tick % BALL_LIGHT_BREATH_PERIOD;
-            if (phase < (BALL_LIGHT_BREATH_PERIOD / 2U))
-            {
-                return (uint8_t)((phase * 255U) / (BALL_LIGHT_BREATH_PERIOD / 2U));
-            }
-            return (uint8_t)(((BALL_LIGHT_BREATH_PERIOD - phase) * 255U) / (BALL_LIGHT_BREATH_PERIOD / 2U));
-
-        default:
-            return 0U;
+        HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_CLK_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
+        HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_SDI_Pin, (output & bit) != 0U ? GPIO_PIN_SET : GPIO_PIN_RESET);
+        HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_CLK_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
     }
+    HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_CLK_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
+    HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_LE_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
+    HAL_GPIO_WritePin(GPIOB, Channel_LE_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
 }

-static uint8_t BallLight_ScaleColor(uint8_t value, uint8_t relative)
-{
-    uint32_t scaled;
-
-    scaled = (uint32_t)value * relative * BallLightness;
-    scaled /= (255U * 10U);
-    if (scaled > 255U)
-    {
-        scaled = 255U;
-    }
-
-    return (uint8_t)scaled;
-}
-
-static uint16_t BallLight_DmaEncodeByte(uint16_t offset, uint8_t data)
-{
-    uint8_t mask;
-
-    for (mask = 0x80U; mask != 0U; mask >>= 1U)
-    {
-        BallLightPwmBuffer[offset++] = (data & mask) != 0U ? BALL_WS2812_CODE_1 : BALL_WS2812_CODE_0;
-    }
-    return offset;
-}
-
-static void BallLight_DmaIdle(void)
-{
-    HAL_TIM_PWM_Stop_DMA(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1);
-    __HAL_TIM_SET_COMPARE(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1, 0U);
-    __HAL_TIM_SET_COUNTER(&htim3, 0U);
-    htim3.Instance->EGR = TIM_EGR_UG;
-    /* 完成/错误后恢复零占空比输出，空闲期间 PA6 保持低电平。 */
-    if (HAL_TIM_PWM_Start(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1) != HAL_OK)
-        Error_Handler();
-    BallLightDmaBusy = 0U;
-}
-
-static bool BallLight_DmaFlush(uint32_t tick)
-{
-    uint8_t i;
-    uint8_t relative;
-    RGB_t color;
-    uint16_t offset = 1U;
-
-    /* DMA 空闲时才编码完整 GRB 帧，传输中不改缓冲区，串口中断始终保持开启。 */
-    for (i = 0U; i < BALL_LED_COUNT; i++)
-    {
-        relative = BallLight_GetRelativeLightness(BallLedMode[i], tick);
-        color = Color_table[BallLedColor[i]];
-
-        offset = BallLight_DmaEncodeByte(offset, BallLight_ScaleColor(color.G, relative));
-        offset = BallLight_DmaEncodeByte(offset, BallLight_ScaleColor(color.R, relative));
-        offset = BallLight_DmaEncodeByte(offset, BallLight_ScaleColor(color.B, relative));
-    }
-
-    /* 首位及尾部保持零占空比；重新装载预装寄存器，避免上一帧 CCR 残值影响起始位。 */
-    HAL_TIM_PWM_Stop(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1);
-    __HAL_TIM_SET_COMPARE(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1, 0U);
-    __HAL_TIM_SET_COUNTER(&htim3, 0U);
-    htim3.Instance->EGR = TIM_EGR_UG;
-    __HAL_TIM_CLEAR_FLAG(&htim3, TIM_FLAG_CC1 | TIM_FLAG_UPDATE);
-    BallLightDmaBusy = 1U;
-    if (HAL_TIM_PWM_Start_DMA(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1,
-                              (uint32_t *)BallLightPwmBuffer, BALL_WS2812_PWM_SIZE) != HAL_OK)
-    {
-        BallLight_DmaIdle();
-        return false;
-    }
-    return true;
-}
-
-void HAL_TIM_PWM_PulseFinishedCallback(TIM_HandleTypeDef *htim)
-{
-    if (htim == &htim3 && htim->Channel == HAL_TIM_ACTIVE_CHANNEL_1)
-    {
-        /* HAL 已确认 DMA 完成，尾部复位低电平也已发送，直接释放发送状态供下一帧使用。 */
-        BallLight_DmaIdle();
-    }
-}
-
-void HAL_TIM_ErrorCallback(TIM_HandleTypeDef *htim)
+void Light_Init(void)
 {
-    if (htim == &htim3 && htim->Channel == HAL_TIM_ACTIVE_CHANNEL_1)
-    {
-        /* DMA 错误后解除忙状态，并让主循环重新发送，避免灯珠刷新永久停住。 */
-        BallLightDmaRetry = 1U;
-        BallLight_DmaIdle();
-    }
+    BallLight_SetChannels(0U);
 }

 void Light_Task(void)
 {
-    static uint32_t LastRefreshTick = 0U;
-    uint32_t CurrentTick;
-    uint8_t DynamicMode = 0U;
-    uint8_t i;
-
-    CurrentTick = HAL_GetTick();
-
-    if (BallLightDmaBusy != 0U)
-        return;
-    if (BallLightDmaRetry != 0U)
-    {
-        BallLightDmaRetry = 0U;
-        BallLightDirty = 1U;
-    }
-
-    for (i = 0U; i < BALL_LED_COUNT; i++)
-    {
-        if (BallLedMode[i] == BALL_MODE_BLINK || BallLedMode[i] == BALL_MODE_BREATH)
-        {
-            DynamicMode = 1U;
-            break;
-        }
-    }
-
-    if (BallLightDirty == 0U && DynamicMode == 0U)
-    {
-        return;
-    }
-
-    if (BallLightDirty == 0U && (CurrentTick - LastRefreshTick) < BALL_LIGHT_REFRESH_INTERVAL)
-    {
-        return;
-    }
-
-    if (BallLight_DmaFlush(CurrentTick) == false)
-        return;
-    BallLightDirty = 0U;
-    LastRefreshTick = CurrentTick;
+    /* 静态通道灯仅在收到新掩码时移位，不需要RGB刷新任务。 */
 }
```

## 49. Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/MainTask.c

文件：`E:\单片机项目\ChatGPT单片机项目\新弹珠机\Wusong_Ball\Task\Src\MainTask.c`

模块/函数：主任务 / Main_Init、Main_Task、SystemLight_Task。

修改原因：运行通信、输入、灯与定时阀，启用PA1板载指示灯。

影响范围：替代模板RGB/数码管任务。

原代码 / 修改后代码：

```diff
diff --git a/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/MainTask.c b/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/MainTask.c
index 03d665c..9ccf4ad 100644
--- a/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/MainTask.c
+++ b/Wusong_Ball/Task/Src/MainTask.c
@@ -3,7 +3,6 @@
 #include "InterruptTask.h"
 #include "KeyTask.h"
 #include "LightTask.h"
-#include "DigitalTubeTask.h"
 #include "port_event.h"
 #include "iwdg.h"

@@ -23,7 +22,7 @@ static void SystemLight_Task(void)

     if ((uint32_t)(HAL_GetTick() - time) > SYSLIGHT_BLINK_TIME)
     {
-        /* 中文注释：球盘User_Led实际接PA15，每500ms翻转一次GPIO。 */
+        /* 中文注释：微动板User_Led实际接PA1，每500ms翻转一次GPIO。 */
         HAL_GPIO_TogglePin(User_Led_GPIO_Port, User_Led_Pin);
         time = HAL_GetTick();
     }
@@ -31,11 +30,11 @@ static void SystemLight_Task(void)

 void Main_Init(void)
 {
-    /* 中文注释：球盘只初始化主板通信、7路光眼、5颗RGB灯和两块外接数码管。 */
+    /* 微动板只运行串口、12路微动、2路洞口、通道灯和3路定时阀。 */
     CommInit();
     KeyAll_Init();
     Light_Init();
-    DigitalTubeTask_Init();
+
 }

 void Main_Task(void)
@@ -46,7 +45,7 @@ void Main_Task(void)
     HAL_IWDG_Refresh(&hiwdg);
     Light_Task();
     HAL_IWDG_Refresh(&hiwdg);
-    DigitalTube_Task();
+    BallValve_Task();
     HAL_IWDG_Refresh(&hiwdg);
-    // SystemLight_Task();
+    SystemLight_Task();
 }
```
